It evaluates theorem proving, solver engineering, and constructions alongside direct attempts to resolve open conjectures, capturing both concrete research accomplishments and progress toward long-standing research goals. TCSExam-Full contains 100 expert-reviewed tasks, with submissions checked by the Lean kernel or an executable task checker. TCSExam-Lite selects 20 tasks, five per track, reducing the number of task attempts by 80% at the same per-attempt budget. We evaluate eight agents on Full and nine on Lite. The TCSExam Index combines all four tracks: GPT-6 Astra leads Full (71.9), and Opus 5.5 leads Lite (72.3). Full and Lite rankings are strongly aligned across eight shared agents (Spearman's $ρ=0.93$), supporting Lite as a compact, discriminative benchmark. The evaluations yield improved solver outputs and mathematical constructions, together with independently verified special cases and bounds from open-conjecture attempts. TCSExam provides an extensible framework with task sources, Docker build recipes, and evaluation tools for reproducible use.

</details>


### [217] [Secure Formal Raising: Protecting the Development of AI through Executable Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08042)
*Rolf Drechsler,Christina Plump*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这篇论文扩大了之前关于机器智能的观点，提出了一种确保提升环境安全性的新方法。SFR通过四值证据体系来维持不确定和矛盾的评估，从而保护策略完整性和授权，并验证了六个安全性不变量，证明了只有经过确认的证据才能被接受。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为扩展模型提升的概念，特别是将安全考虑融入其中，应对提升过程中可能遇到的策略完整性、授权以及敏感信息流动的风险。

Method: 论文提出了 Secure Formal Raising (SFR)，引入了四值证据体系，这是基于正式提升（FR）的方法的扩展。SFR旨在保护提升环境的四大方面：策略完整性、授权、保密数据流动，以及通过使用四值证据来处理不确定性和不一致性。

Result: 论文详细证明了SFR的六个安全性不变量，展示了这些不变量如何确保仅采用确认证据。通过实证分析发现，虽然初步验证满足了安全性不变量，但工具使用中存在混乱代理人漏洞，这一问题通过证明原出处的要求得到了解决。

Conclusion: SFR为避免在模型提升过程中出现的安全漏洞提供了一种解决方案，提升了机器智能在复杂动态环境中的稳健性和可靠性。

Abstract: Model raising revisits Turing's developmental view of machine intelligence. Formal Raising (FR) has recently been proposed to complement this view with explicit behavioral properties. This paper extends that proposal to the security of the raising environment. Secure Formal Raising (SFR) models policy integrity, authorization, and confidential data flow using four-valued evidence that preserves unknown and inconsistent assessments. We prove six security invariants and show that they force admission to accept only confirmed evidence. A plain-C reference implementation satisfies the invariants in a bounded exhaustive exploration, which also exposes a confused-deputy gap for tool calls that a provenance requirement closes.

</details>


### [218] [When Imperfect Automated Fact-Checking Backfires: Social Learning in the Presence of Misinformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08053)
*Zixuan Liu,Ming Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了自动化事实检查工具在社交媒体上如何影响信息准确性的传播。通过模型分析，发现自动化工具虽然可以避免错误传播，但错误也会误导用户，尤其在文章可靠性适中、人工检查成本高且真实信息不具有特别重要性时，自动化工具会降低总体福利。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鉴于自动化事实检查工具在社交媒体上的广泛应用可能存在的潜在问题，研究其社会影响以及不同的使用情景下的效果对于提高信息传播的准确性和减少误导性的信息传播至关重要。

Method: 研究采用顺序社会学习模型，模拟了用户在面对含有潜在不准确信息的文章时的决策过程。测试了在不同条件下自动化工具的使用效率。

Result: 研究证明，在某些情况下，自动化工具会降低社会学习系统的整体福利。主要原因在于其可能引入错误，导致用户受到误导，尤其是在文章可靠性适中且人工检查成本较高的情况下。

Conclusion: 文章结论显示，对于某些具体条件，自动化事实检查工具可能会不利，而仅使用人工检查可能更有效。在特定情境下，自动化工具带来的帮助是有限的，取决于其准确性和文章的重要性等变量。

Abstract: Automated fact-checking tools, cheap and fast but imperfect, are increasingly available on social platforms. We study when such tools can backfire in a sequential social learning model of misinformation. Agents observe an article's reliability and their predecessors' actions, which indicate whether they liked or disliked the article. They may also pay to fact-check it. We examine how welfare, defined as the probability of a correct cascade, changes when a free, error-prone automated tool supplements costly but perfectly accurate manual fact-checking. Two opposing effects arise. Broader use of fact-checking helps agents escape wrong cascades. At the same time, errors from the automated tool can mislead agents who would otherwise have verified the article perfectly, reducing the likelihood of correct cascades. We prove that adding the tool strictly lowers welfare for articles of intermediate reliability when truthful articles are not too informative and manual checking is sufficiently costly. Simulations show that the tool can help on either side of the intermediate range, and that it improves on manual-only checking on average only above a threshold accuracy.

</details>


### [219] [DictLLM: Post-training Compression of Large Language Model with Dictionary Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08082)
*Jinho Park,Se Young Chun,Mingoo Seok*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过提出基于字典的压缩方案DictLLM，本文在不牺牲性能的情况下，显著减少了大语言模型在移动设备上的存储需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在移动设备上部署大型语言模型时，需要平衡性能与计算和内存的限制。现有的后训练低秩压缩方法效果有限，因此本文提出了一种新的压缩方案DictLLM。

Method: DictLLM 是一个基于字典的压缩方案，首先通过 SVD 获取共享字典，然后稀疏化层特定系数，进一步优化字典并使用因子特定的海森矩阵信息进行量化。

Result: 在语言建模和零样本推理基准测试中，DictLLM相比现有后训练低秩压缩方法性能更优。将原始 LLAMA-7B 模型压缩了5.1倍，仅增加了2.9点的WikiText-2困惑度，并实现了比最先进的低秩方法高三倍的压缩率。

Conclusion: 通过DictLLM，本文展示了如何在不牺牲性能的情况下显著减少大语言模型在移动设备上的存储需求。

Abstract: Efficient deployment of large language models (LLMs) on mobile devices requires balancing performance with compute and memory constraints. Post-training compression is an effective approach to managing this trade-off, while requiring much less computation than in-training counterparts. We propose DictLLM, a dictionary-based compression scheme in which weight matrices within grouped layers are decomposed into a shared dictionary and layer-specific sparse coefficients, directly obtained from a pre-trained model without further training. Specifically, we cast DictLLM as a four-step post-training optimization. First, we obtain shared dictionaries via singular value decomposition (SVD) of input-scaled weight matrices, amortizing rank across grouped layers; second, we sparsify the layer-specific coefficients to the target compression ratio using a dictionary-aware Hessian; and third, we refine the dictionaries given the resulting sparse coefficients. Finally, we aggressively quantize both components using factor-specific Hessian information: input covariance for the dictionaries and the dictionary-aware Hessian for the coefficients. The resulting representation reduces memory footprint by storing a single shared dictionary per layer group, along with sparse layer-specific coefficients. On language modeling and zero-shot reasoning benchmarks, DictLLM consistently outperforms existing post-training low-rank compression methods and pushes the Pareto frontier between model size and performance. DictLLM compresses the original LLaMA-7B model by 5.1x with only a 2.9-point increase in WikiText-2 perplexity, achieving a 3.6x higher compression ratio than the state-of-the-art low-rank method.

</details>


### [220] [MUSS: A Multi-scale and Sequence-based Model for Single-cell Gene Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08086)
*Kangjie Zheng,Amirhossein Vahidi,Liying Jin,Joseph Clarke,Vijaya Baskar MS,Connor Rogerson,Mengji Zhang,Muzlifah Haniffa,Anshul Kundaje,Mo Lotfollahi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MUSS 是一种多尺度的序列基模型，用于单细胞基因调控，它通过固定预训练的序列编码器获取峰值和基因级表示，并通过峰值-峰值自注意力和距离偏置峰值-基因互动整合它们。MUSS 在基因表达预测、增强子-基因连接、基于基序的调节优先级确定和零样本细胞聚类方面表现优异，超过了序列基和单细胞组学基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基因调控建模方法要么侧重于DNA层面而缺乏细胞层面的分辨率，要么专注于固定的基因和峰词汇而忽略了它们的底层序列信息。为了克服这两个限制，研究者提出了MUSS模型。

Method: MUSS 在DNA上使用固定的预训练序列编码器获得峰和基因级表示，并通过峰值-峰值自注意力和距离偏置的峰值-基因互动进行整合。通过遮罩语言建模及其与scATAC-seq和scRNA-seq配对的数据训练，使这些表示与细胞特异性调节状态对齐。

Result: MUSS 在基因表达预测、增强子-基因连接、基于基序的调节优先级确定和零样本细胞聚类中表现出色，并且能够预测在任务训练期间未见过的基因和峰值的表达。

Conclusion: MUSS 证明了基于序列的多尺度表示方法对于解码细胞类型特异性调节计划的强大性。

Abstract: Modeling gene regulation in single cells requires jointly representing DNA sequence, chromatin accessibility, and gene expression across regulatory scales. Sequence-based models capture DNA-level regulatory grammar but often lack cell-level resolution, whereas many single-cell omics models represent genes and peaks as fixed-vocabulary tokens without their underlying sequence. Here, we present MUSS, a sequence-based multi-scale model for single-cell gene regulation that preserves both DNA-level and cell-level resolution. MUSS derives peak- and gene-level representations from DNA using a frozen pre-trained sequence encoder and integrates them through peak-peak self-attention and distance-biased peak-gene interactions. Peak masked language modeling followed by training on paired scATAC-seq and scRNA-seq aligns these representations with cell-specific regulatory states. Across gene expression prediction, enhancer-gene linking, motif-guided regulatory prioritization, and zero-shot cell clustering, MUSS achieves strong performance against sequence-based and single-cell omics baselines. It further predicts expression for genes and peaks unseen during task training. These results support sequence-grounded, multi-scale representations for decoding cell-type-specific regulatory programs. Code, datasets, and model checkpoints are available at https://github.com/Lotfollahi-lab/MUSS_neurips26.

</details>


### [221] [Create Your Own Work Stack: Deploying Self-hosted AI and Tooling in a Tech Startup](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08116)
*Saad Ahmed Bazaz,Talha Amjad,Abdurrehman Subhani,Sohaib Ahmed Bazaz,Abdullah Aamir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 部署本地自托管AI节省了部分费用，通过本地模型处理低复杂度任务，降低了30-40%的商业AI工作量，但仍需优化用户体验以实现全面采用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减轻商业AI的高昂成本，特别是在小型AI密集型软件公司中。

Method: 采用商业和本地模型的混合架构，将低复杂度任务工作转移至本地模型。

Result: 约30-40%的商业AI工作量可以节省，尤其是操作协调方面。

Conclusion: 自托管AI最适合作为商业AI的混合架构的一部分，基于任务需求、成本、上下文需求和操作敏感度分配任务。

Abstract: Self-hosting AI is often presented as a way to reduce inference costs and increase control, but deployment introduces its own costs. We report our experience deploying a hybrid AI stack at Grayhat Developers, a small software company with an AI-intensive development workflow. Our initial motivation was to counter the growing cost of commercial AI usage: Claude/Codex incurred approximately \$325 per month, with more than 25.93 billion recorded tokens. We experimented with local open-source models as replacements for commercial coding agents, but after learning that their performance and inference speed were insufficient, we adopted a hybrid approach in which commercial agents remained responsible for complex tasks while local models handled lower-complexity tasks, organizational information retrieval, and synchronization with self-hosted tools including Outline, Plane, and ERPNext.
  We estimated that approximately 30--40\% of our commercial AI workload was associated with context transfer, documentation synchronization, task management, and related operational coordination. By moving this work to local models, we estimate that approximately 90\% of this coordination workload could be handled without commercial inference, corresponding to an estimated 27--36\% reduction in overall commercial-AI workload or cost-equivalent usage. Our first internal deployment nevertheless failed to achieve adoption because it introduced UX friction. Replacing it with a centrally configured deployment substantially improved feedback and adoption. We conclude that local inference is most useful not as a wholesale replacement for commercial AI, but as part of a hybrid architecture that assigns tasks according to their capability requirements, cost, context demands, and operational sensitivity.

</details>


### [222] [A Generative Foundation Model for Multimodal Tokamak Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08153)
*Yingshuai Ji,Haodong Feng,Guohong Deng,Gelin Bian,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过扩展TokaMark基准，增加两类任务（反向动作和预测破坏），提出了一个生成式托卡马克基础模型GenToka，能够在任务特定适应下，共享和跨任务的结构信息，更有效地处理托卡马克实验中的多模态观测数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型大多针对单一任务和固定输入输出，且依赖特定诊断信息，无法全面提取多模态观测数据的全局、跨信号信息。因此，有必要开发一个可重用的基础模型，该模型能够适应不同任务的输入输出模式，并能联合建模异构目标信号。

Method: 研究通过将TokaMark基准扩展为多个小组任务，引入了一个名为GenToka的生成式托卡马克基础模型。该模型采用共享条件扩散Transformer生成由输入数据选定的目标，具备特定信号编码器保持原始时序、剖面、场和标量信息的能力。此外，基础预训练和任务特定适应传递了跨信号结构，在六个小组的所有任务中。

Result: 实验结果表明，GenToka相比于TokaMind在1-4组的宏观平均归一化均方根误差降低了4.7%，在第5组降低了17.7%，在第6组则在共同验证下阈值提高了8.4%的窗口级F1值。

Conclusion: 研究证明了GenToka模型作为实验托卡马克的可重用、生成式和多模态途径的有效性，能够连接等离子体状态重构、反向动作推断和破坏警告，以支持更全面的、基于数据的等离子体预测与控制研究。

Abstract: Tokamak experiments record diverse measurements, including waveforms, profiles, fields, and scalars, which are used to reconstruct the plasma, forecast its evolution, recover associated control signals, and estimate disruption risk. Most models are built separately for one task and a fixed set of inputs and outputs, and each relies on a prescribed subset of diagnostics rather than structuring the full multimodal observation set to extract global, cross-signal information about the discharge. These limitations motivate a reusable foundation model that adapts to each task's input-output schema and models heterogeneous target signals jointly, rather than retraining a separate model for every configuration. In this work, we extend the TokaMark benchmark with two groups of tasks for actuator action inversion and disruption prediction, yielding 20 MAST tasks in six groups with explicit schemas and evaluation protocols. Then, we introduce GenToka, a generative tokamak foundation model that jointly generates schema-selected targets through a shared conditional diffusion Transformer, with signal-specific codecs preserving native time series, profiles, fields, and scalars; foundation pretraining and task-specific adaptation transfer cross-signal structure across all six groups. Evaluation on MAST measurements shows that GenToka reduces macro-average normalized root mean squared error relative to TokaMind by 4.7% on Groups 1-4 and by 17.7% on Group 5, and improves macro-average window-level F1 on Group 6 by 8.4% at a common validation-selected threshold. GenToka provides a reusable, generative, and multimodal pathway for experimental tokamaks, linking plasma-state reconstruction, actuator inference, and disruption warning from shared measurements to support more integrated, data-driven fusion plasma prediction and control research.

</details>


### [223] [PCBRouteAgent: How Close Are LLM Agents to Becoming PCB Routing Engineers?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08175)
*Yue Zhang,Jilei Sun,Liqiang Jing,Yijie Wei,Ruohan Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PCBRouteAgent是一个结合全局策略和局部执行的PCB路由代理，能够在复杂板上实现可靠的自动线路布设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化PCB路由工具在处理复杂电路板时仍面临挑战，PCBRouteAgent旨在通过结合人类工程师的全局策略和局部执行方式来克服这些挑战。

Method: PCBRouteAgent通过Global Routing Planner和Detailed Routing Executor两个模块实现。前者使用板图像和结构化的电气、几何信息来规划连接拓扑、布线顺序、通道和层切换，后者则通过KiCad的本地布线引擎实现这些指导，并进行局部恢复。

Result: PCBRouteAgent在247个合成和实际电路板上进行了测试，实现了100%、94.9%、100%和40%不等的pass率，并在三个实际电路板子集上超越了其他评估基准，分别高出13.9、36和10个百分点。

Conclusion: PCBRouteAgent为解决复杂PCB的可靠自动化布线提供了一种有效方法，表明结合全局策略和局部执行可以显著提高自动布线的质量和成功率。

Abstract: Automating printed circuit board (PCB) routing requires coordinating board-level connectivity decisions with geometric execution under strict design rules. Although recent agents can interact with native routing tools, reliable routing on complex boards remains challenging. We present PCBRouteAgent, a multimodal PCB routing agent inspired by how human engineers coordinate global strategy with local execution. A Global Routing Planner combines board images and structured electrical and geometric information to specify connection topology, routing order, corridors, and layer transitions. A Detailed Routing Executor realizes these guides through KiCad's native routing engine and performs bounded local recovery. Unresolved failures are returned as structured feedback, enabling targeted guide revisions while preserving accepted routes. We evaluate PCBRouteAgent on 247 synthetic and real-world boards from four PCBWorld-Bench test splits. It achieves clean-pass rates of 100%, 94.9%, 100%, and 40% on D2, D3-A, D3-B, and D3-C, respectively. On the three real-world subsets, PCBRouteAgent outperforms the best evaluated baseline in clean-pass rate by 13.9, 36, and 10 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [224] [BabyTheorist: A Benchmark for Learning to Theorize the World from Observation Alone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08177)
*Doojin Baek,Junyeob Baek,Mingyu Jo,Hosung Lee,Taegu Kang,Gyubin Lee,Sungjin Ahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文引入了一个名为BabyTheorist的新基准，旨在评估机器在纯观察下学习和应用理论的能力。该基准通过生成连续的前后视觉观察，以及控制程序语言、复杂度递增的课程、常见的子程序和持续的训练测试协议来实现目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的视觉推理基准主要侧重于基于共同规则的少量样本的拼图问题，而忽略了从观察中学习通用理论的能力。论文提出了一个新的基准来专门测试这一能力。

Method: 通过生成随机组合程序的前后视觉观察，该基准使学习者只能看到过渡信息，而没有程序标签、任务标识或支持集。此外，它通过控制程序语言、复杂度递增的课程、常见的子程序和持续的训练测试协议来支持理论学习和评估。

Result: 七种代表性的基线算法在该基准上表现不佳，尤其是在课程深化后差距更大。这表明现有的程序学习方法尚未解决这种能力需求。

Conclusion: BabyTheorist为评估机器学习从纯观察中获取和应用理论的能力提供了一个可控的环境，证明了这是传统方法无法解决的一个重要方向。

Abstract: How would we know whether a machine truly understands the world? We argue that one key capability is \textit{observational theory learning}: acquiring reusable theories from pure observations and applying them to new situations. Current visual reasoning benchmarks focus primarily on the latter, presenting each task as a few-shot puzzle grouped by a common rule. We introduce BabyTheorist, a benchmark for controlled training and evaluation of observational theory learning. BabyTheorist generates continual streams of before-after visual observations from hidden compositional programs; during training, learners see only the resulting transitions, with no program labels, task identities, or support sets. To support theory learning and evaluation, BabyTheorist controls the program language, a curriculum over program complexity, recurring frequent subprograms, and a continual train-test protocol with level skipping. Seven representative baselines plateau well below ceiling, with the gap widening as the curriculum advances---evidence that BabyTheorist isolates a capability current program-learning approaches do not yet address.

</details>


### [225] [SMART: State-based Multi Agent Reasoning Traces for Verification-oriented Problem-Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08196)
*Sai Vallurupalli,Francis Ferraro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 文章提出了一个名为SMART的多智能体框架，通过捕捉问题上下文中参与实体的隐性状态来获取知识，从而进行知识引导的解决方案推理，提高了复杂科学声明的验证性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文章针对复杂的、缺乏足够背景信息的问题（如声明）提出了SMART框架，旨在通过获取相关背景知识来改善解决方案的推理过程。

Method: SMART框架采用了多智能体结构，利用各种LLMs和基于角色的结构化提示策略来捕获实体的隐性状态，并通过现实世界验证的先验知识来进行知识引导的解决方案推理。

Result: 实验表明，SMART框架在三个数据集上的复杂科学声明验证性能相比之前提高了20%，通过将前提条件置于现实世界知识中进一步提升性能5%。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了将前提条件与现实世界知识相结合可以显著提高复杂声明验证的任务性能，展示了SMART框架的有效性及实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Complex problems with limited context, such as claims, require a means to obtain additional knowledge necessary for solution reasoning. We propose a multi-agent framework, SMART, to obtain knowledge through capturing the implicit state of participating entities in a problem context. SMART curates the state knowledge as diverse, complementary and solution-salient premises using a variety of LLMs and a structured role-based prompt strategy. Using knowledge from these real-world verified curated premises, SMART's knowledge guided solution reasoning improves validation performance up to 20% on complex scientific claims spanning 3 datasets. We show that grounding premises in real-world knowledge is necessary and improves performance up to 5%.

</details>


### [226] [Signals, Evidence, and Context: User Scrutiny in Real-World LLM Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08232)
*Ahana Biswas,Yu-Ru Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究了大型语言模型（LLMs）的回答特征如何影响用户接受、检查和质疑行为，发现虽然有31.9%的回答包含广泛表述，但仅有1.33%的回答包含显式的URL链接。确认了证据的可检视性、不确定性及其他回应提示对检查和质疑的影响，并揭示了某些对话中的细致审查集中在特定会话。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究大型语言模型的回答特征以了解用户如何接受、检查和质疑这些回答，旨在提高模型回答的透明性和用户信任。

Method: 通过分析来自ShareChat和WildChat的489,283个辅助用户响应对，识别回答中的广泛表述和显式URL，对Technical Help和Seeking Information两类场景进行了详细分析。

Result: 广泛表述出现的比例较高，但显式URL较少，确认了证据可检视性的重要性，不确定性与检查相关，但未明确支持检查多于质疑。在不同场景中，不确定性和拒绝理由的细节影响着检查与质疑的倾向。

Conclusion: 提高大型语言模型回答的证据检视性、考虑用户试图解决的问题以及对话历史对于增强用户验证信任至关重要。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often present synthesized answers without making their supporting evidence easy to inspect. We study how features of these answers relate to users' subsequent accepting, checking, and challenging in 489,283 assistant-user response pairs from ShareChat and WildChat. Broad attribution language appears in 31.9% of qualifying responses, compared with explicit URLs in 1.33%, revealing a gap between claims of grounding and preserved inspectable support. Attribution, uncertainty, and other response cues generally accompany both checking and challenging; only uncertainty shows a small statistically distinguishable pooled association favoring checking. Within Technical Help, uncertainty favors checking relative to challenging; refusals and reasoning traces favor challenging in Seeking Information. Scrutiny is concentrated in some conversations, while its apparent turn-to-turn persistence becomes much smaller when conversation-level differences and response ordering are examined separately. These results suggest that supporting verification requires attention to what evidence can be inspected, which question a user is trying to resolve, and the history of the interaction.

</details>


### [227] [Value-oriented policy distillation for interpretable wind farm power control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08245)
*Max Bruninx,Timothy Verstraeten,Jan Decuyper,Ann Nowé,Jan Helsen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过使用可解释的提升树（EBMs）来生成透明的控制策略，该研究提出了一个策略蒸馏框架，并结合能源预测领域的研究成果引入了价值导向的视角和专家知识，从而实现了在实际风力发电场中减少不平衡成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了改进风力发电场的功率控制，使其能自动响应不平衡价格，减少因预测误差导致的不平衡成本，同时保持系统的稳定运行。

Method: 利用提升树（EBMs）进行策略蒸馏，结合专家知识和价值导向的方法来生成可解释的控制策略。

Result: 研究提出的基于价值导向和专家知识的控制器能够在实际比利时电网和离岸风力发电场中将不平衡成本降低82%，并在性能上优于阈值定价控制器及基于决策树的策略蒸馏基准。

Conclusion: 所提出的策略蒸馏框架不仅能提高系统的经济性，还能增强控制策略的可解释性和可验证性，有助于实现合理风能管理。

Abstract: Wind farm power control can help the system maintain balance and avoid blackouts. In Europe, this can be achieved through implicit balancing, where a wind farm adjusts its power setpoint in anticipation of the imbalance price using information published by the Transmission System Operator. Thereby, the wind farm helps restore system balance while also reducing its own imbalance costs resulting from forecast errors. However, implicit balancing is a challenging problem since the settlement price is unknown in advance and can be impacted by the pursued control strategy. Reinforcement learning methods could help to solve this problem, but lack the interpretability needed to be deployed on real wind farms. Therefore, we propose a policy distillation framework using explainable boosting machines (EBMs), a variant of generalized additive models, to obtain a transparent control policy. To increase the value captured by the EBM model, we introduce a value-oriented perspective to policy distillation inspired by research in the field of energy forecasting. Furthermore, we suggest to include expert knowledge during model design, such that the control strategy is also justifiable to stakeholders. Using real data of the Belgian grid and an offshore wind farm, we find that our value-oriented and expert-based controller can reduce imbalance costs by 82%, while outperforming a price threshold controller as well as a policy distillation baseline using decision trees.

</details>


### [228] [The Geometry of Simulated Perspectives: Dissociating Representation and Behaviour in a Multimodal Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08262)
*Mathis Immertreu,Fitim Abdullahu,Thomas Kinfe,Claus Metzner,Helmut Grabner,Patrick Krauss,Achim Schilling*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct在不同人设条件下对100张图像的处理，发现早期层主要编码图像身份，晚期层则展现人设特定结构，而提示框架比人设身份更深刻地重塑内部表示，但人设内容更多影响行为判断，表明代表距离不一定反映行为相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨模拟视角如何重塑多模态语言模型的内部表示及其对判断的影响。

Method: 使用Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct模型，在100张图像和53种引导条件下系统地改变人设内容和引入方式，同时记录行为评估和层内隐藏状态。

Result: 早期层主要编码图像身份，晚期层展现人设特定结构。提示框架比人设身份更大幅度地重塑内部表示，但人设内容更多影响行为判断。这种分离由即使不做响应改变也能改变表示的最小身份陈述进一步说明。

Conclusion: 研究表明，代表距离不一定反映行为相关性。同时揭示了模拟视角如何在多模态语言模型的表征空间内部展现为不同的结构，强调了内部表示空间的几何结构和功能后果之间的根本区别。

Abstract: Multimodal language models can simulate different observers, yet it remains unclear how assigned perspectives reshape their internal representations and whether these changes govern their judgements. Here, we investigate the representational geometry of persona-conditioned visual interestingness in Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct. Across 100 images and 53 prompting conditions, we systematically vary persona content and the linguistic framing used to introduce it, while recording behavioural ratings and layer-wise hidden states. We observe a systematic reorganisation of representational geometry across processing depth: early layers primarily encode image identity, whereas persona-specific structures emerge in later layers. Strikingly, prompt framing reshapes internal representations more strongly than persona identity, whereas persona content has a greater impact on behavioural judgements. This dissociation is further illustrated by minimal identity statements that alter representations even without changing responses. These findings demonstrate that representational distance does not necessarily reflect behavioural relevance. More broadly, they reveal how simulated perspectives emerge as distinct structures within the representational space of artificial cognitive systems, highlighting a fundamental distinction between the geometry of internal representations and their functional consequences.

</details>


### [229] [LeanContext: Disentangling Mathematical Resources from Local Formal Context in Lean Whole-Proof Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08277)
*Hang Zhang,Qihao Ye,Litong Liu,Yunbei Zhang,Yugyung Lee,Yue Zhou,Bowen Liu,Emily First,Cheng Han,Qiao Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 介绍了LeanContext基准，包含129,826个Lean整个证明问题，用于评估神经定理证明中选取前提和上下文环境的效果。通过比较使用数学上下文(MC)和环境上下文(EC)训练的模型表现，发现包含MC的训练能显著改善模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前定理证明系统在处理数学证明时，需要使用到相关前提和上下文信息。然而，这些资源的价值尚未得到充分评估。因此，作者设计了LeanContext基准来探究这些因素的影响。

Method: 构造了一个包含129,826个完整证明问题的基准，将选取的完整前置声明归类为数学上下文(MC)，其余为环境上下文(EC)。通过对比具有MC和EC训练效果的方法，进行交叉训练和推理评估。

Result: 结果表明，带有MC训练模型相比于仅使用EC的模型表现更好，特别是在LLaDA和Llama模型上，Pass@32分别提高了1.40%和1.70%。并且这些实验不依赖于目标证明参考。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，提供完整的数学上下文信息对于提高神经定理证明性能至关重要，同时也为进一步改进相关模型提供了指导。

Abstract: Neural theorem proving increasingly uses retrieved premises and project context to support proof generation. Evaluating these resources requires measuring the advantage of target-selected declarations over comparable alternatives and how this advantage depends on training. We introduce LeanContext, a benchmark of 129,826 Lean whole-proof generation problems, to investigate these questions. We group selected complete preceding mathematical declarations as Mathematical Context (MC) and the remaining selected local source commands as Environment Context (EC). Four aligned input views support crossed training and inference evaluations with shared targets and original Lean verification environments. For both diffusion- and autoregressive-based models, selected declarations outperform comparable alternatives by a larger margin when training includes MC. Final-input audits identify omitted declarations referenced by the target. Prioritizing these dependencies improves Pass@32 by 1.40% and 1.70% for LLaDA and Llama, respectively, on 2,000 targets. The selection experiment operates without access to target reference proofs and holds checkpoints, EC, and inference budgets constant. The code and dataset are available at https://github.com/haaaardcvZ/LeanContext.

</details>


### [230] [A Generative Model of Contextual Integrity: Appropriate vs. Inappropriate Information Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08284)
*Omer Ebead,Claude Formanek,Joel Z. Leibo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究通过模拟对话构建生成模型来探讨情境规范下的信息共享，发现信息在恰当的上下文中被分享，但也可能被错误地披露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨如何在不同的情境下根据情境规范进行适当的信息分享，以及在信息交流过程中，信息持有人的决策逻辑、态度和语言模型特性如何影响信息分享与拒绝行为。

Method: 利用大型语言模型构建模拟对话的生成模型，设计了两个角色进行信息交换的场景。

Result: 研究结果表明，信息在需要时被正确分享，但在某些情况下也可能被错误披露给非正当接收者。

Conclusion: 该模型为研究情境变化下信息分享和拒绝的发展提供了新的视角和方法。

Abstract: Contextual integrity (CI) holds that appropriate information flow depends on context-specific norms. The same disclosure may be appropriate in one context and inappropriate in another. We build a generative model of contextual integrity to study information sharing through simulated conversations. Two actors played by large language models exchange messages, with one holding private information and the other seeking it. We examine how sharing and refusal vary with the situation, the holder's decision logic, disposition, and the underlying language model. Actors shared information with the rightful recipients when it was needed, but also disclosed it to people who were not entitled to receive it. The model provides a way to study how disclosure and refusal develop through conversation as the situations and the actors vary.

</details>


### [231] [First-Token Attraction in Mamba Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08303)
*Trinh T. Nguyen,Minh-Khoi Nguyen-Nhat,Duy-Tung Pham,Hoang-Son Do,Tan Minh Nguyen,Thieu N. Vo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究分析了深Mamba模型中的token动态，特别是第一token的吸引作用。通过引入高维不变框和尖锐退出时间论证的新分析框架，证明了token轨迹可能的极限点，并证明了指数收敛速率。实验验证了第一token在深度增加时的吸积效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度Mamba模型中的token动态分析尚未形成系统的理论框架，特别是对于第一token在动态中的角色理解不足，而现有的针对基于注意力机制模型的技术在此无效。

Method: 研究引入了基于高维不变框和尖锐退出时间论证的新分析框架，该框架能部分恢复token动态中的符号定性，从而分析token轨迹的极限点，以及证明收敛速率。

Result: 通过该分析框架，研究成功证明了token轨迹可能的极限点，并证明了指数收敛速率。实验表明，在Falcon-Mamba-7B模型中，第一token的吸积效应随深度增加而增大，并在后续层级中占据主导地位。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了第一token在深Mamba模型中的特殊动态特征，其对后续token具有显著的吸引力，导致token聚类和注意力陷阱现象的出现。该发现也扩展到了更广泛的时间序列模型中。

Abstract: We study token dynamics in deep Mamba models, with a particular focus on the attracting role of the first token. Unlike attention mechanisms, where token interactions are governed by positive scalar coefficients, Mamba dynamics involve full matrix-valued interaction coefficients with no positivity or definiteness guarantees. This structural difference makes Mamba dynamics substantially harder to analyze and prevents the direct application of existing techniques for attention-based models. To overcome this challenge, we introduce a new analytical framework based on a high-dimensional invariant box and a sharp exit-time argument, which together recover an effective form of sign-definiteness. Using this framework, we characterize the possible limit points of token trajectories and prove explicit exponential convergence rates. Our analysis reveals that the first token has privileged dynamics: its limiting direction attracts subsequent tokens, inducing token clustering and attention-sink phenomena in deep Mamba models. We further show that this attraction mechanism extends to broader sequence dynamics, including state-space models, causal attention, and classical time-series models. Experiments on Falcon-Mamba-7B validate our predictions, showing that concentration on the first token increases with depth and dominates in later layers.

</details>


### [232] [RM-VLM: From Natural-Language Task Descriptions to Vision-Grounded Reward Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08308)
*Roko Parac,Christos Ziakas,Leo Ardon,Alessandra Russo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种新的方法RM-VLM，它利用大型语言模型和视觉语言模型从自然语言任务描述中生成奖励机器（RM），并在RM引导的策略学习过程中作为标签函数，从而解决了传统RM需要手工工程标签的限制。实验结果显示RM-VLM在非马尔可夫网格导航、机器人操作和3D物体收集等多个领域中均表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的奖励机器（RMs）虽然能处理非马尔可夫任务，但需要手工工程的标签函数，限制了其应用范围。本研究旨在通过结合自然语言处理和视觉文本处理能力，自动生成适用于各种环境的RM，从而更广泛地应用RMs于复杂任务。

Method: 该方法通过大型语言模型生成RM从自然语言任务描述，并利用视觉语言模型作为标签函数，在基于RM的策略学习中进行观测的逻辑门限操作。使用基于VLM的价值函数估算器来评估智能体向RM过渡的进展，并生成密集的奖励信号。该方法被应用于三个不同的任务域：非马尔可夫网格世界导航、机器人操作和3D物体采集。

Result: RM-VLM在不同的任务域中表现出色，成功实现了较高的任务成功率，优于现有的基于VLM的奖励方法。

Conclusion: RM-VLM方法结合了自然语言处理和视觉语言模型的优势，能够自动从自然语言任务描述中生成RM，并在多种非马尔可夫任务中展现出优异的性能，未来可能进一步应用于更复杂的任务环境。

Abstract: Reward machines (RMs) have been proposed as a reward formulation in reinforcement learning (RL) to capture non-Markovian tasks where credit assignment depends on history. By representing reward functions as automata, RMs expose the task's temporal structure and allow such rewards to be handled within a Markovian framework. However, they assume a labelling function, a proposition-grounding oracle for observations, which must be manually engineered for each environment, limiting their wider applicability. In this work, we address this limitation by proposing RM-VLM, a novel approach in which a large language model (LLM) generates an RM from a natural-language task description, and a vision-language model (VLM) acts as the labelling function, grounding the RM's propositions over image-based observations during RM-guided policy learning. A VLM-based value function estimator is used to assess the agent's progress towards each RM transition and derive a dense reward signal. We evaluate RM-VLM on three domains covering non-Markovian grid-world navigation, robotic manipulation, and 3D object collection. Our experimental results show that RM-VLM consistently achieves higher task success rates than existing VLM-based reward methods across all tested domains.

</details>


### [233] [XBridge: Entity-Grounded Latent Bridge for Heterogeneous LLM Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11676)
*Wooseong Yang,Wei-Chieh Huang,Weizhi Zhang,Yu Wang,Philip S. Yu,Junhyun Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: XBRIDGE 是一种无需解码的通信协议，通过 Lexical Anchor Mapping 和 Latent Enrichment Bridge 机制解决了跨架构通信中的实体接地问题。它在多种模型和基准测试中表现出色，降低了延迟并提高了任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多代理LLM系统通信协议要么通过文本传输，遗漏了发送方的内部表示，要么需要架构一致性来实现低级级别的转移。XBRIDGE 旨在解决跨架构通信中的实体接地问题，提高通信质量和效率。

Method: XBRIDGE 采用两种机制来实现跨模型的高效通信：Lexical Anchor Mapping (LAM) 和 Latent Enrichment Bridge (LEB)。LAM 帮助将发送方的原始上下文标记映射到接收方的词汇表中，提供离散的实体锚点。LEB 允许接收方查询发送方的隐藏状态以获取上下文增强。

Result: XBRIDGE 在三个模型家族（Llama、Qwen 和 Mistral）、七个基准测试中表现出色，比基于文本的通信有更高的性能，同时在同架构设置下还优于 KV 共享基线。

Conclusion: XBRIDGE 有效地解决了跨架构通信中的实体接地问题，证明了其在提高多模型系统通信效率和性能方面的潜力。

Abstract: Heterogeneous multi-agent LLM systems, where agents are powered by different model families, can outperform homogeneous configurations by reducing redundant reasoning patterns. Yet existing communication protocols either operate through text, discarding the sender's internal representations, or require architectural homogeneity for latent-level transfer. We identify the entity grounding problem in cross-architecture communication: cross-attention bridges that transfer continuous representations across different LLM families suffer from rare-token compression collapse, where entity identity is lost in the continuous bottleneck (bridge-only F1 ~30%). We propose XBRIDGE, a decode-free communication protocol that addresses this through two mechanisms. Lexical Anchor Mapping (LAM) maps the sender's original context tokens to the receiver's vocabulary, providing discrete entity anchors. A Latent Enrichment Bridge (LEB) lets the receiver query the sender's hidden states for contextual enrichment. The entity anchors ground the bridge's contextual signals to specific entities through the receiver's own self-attention. Across three model families (Llama, Qwen, and Mistral), seven benchmarks, and both communication directions, XBRIDGE outperforms text-based communication on all seven tasks for each model pair while achieving 11x lower latency, and in a same-architecture setting it also exceeds a KV-sharing baseline on six of seven tasks. LEB requires only 264M trainable parameters (3.8% of the receiver), is trained on a small balanced sample set, and adds negligible inference overhead.

</details>


### [234] [Benchmarking Large Language Models on Heterogeneous Cognitive and Biomedical Knowledge Graphs: The Role of Graph Grounding in Multi-Hop Reasoning and Hallucination Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08315)
*Ali Sarabadani,Kheirolah Rahsepar Fard,Tahereh Jeirani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了五种大型语言模型在五个知识图谱上的推理能力，发现通用模型通常优于领域特定模型，知识图谱接地能减少模型的虚构率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估大型语言模型在生物医学和认知神经科学中的推理能力，特别是在复杂知识图谱上的表现。

Method: 采用多级查询数据集评估模型在知识图谱有向和无向条件下的推理深度和事实准确性。

Result: 通用模型（GPT-5.1 和 Gemini 2.0 Pro）在多跳和高级推理任务中普遍优于领域特定模型，但随着查询复杂性的增加，性能下降。知识图谱接地能减少跨评估模型的虚构率。

Conclusion: 研究强调了结合广泛语言模型能力和结构化领域知识的价值，并提供了针对任务复杂性和计算约束的选择LLM-KG集成策略的实际建议，同时也讨论了混合神经象征架构在个性化医疗、认知障碍诊断和解释性AI中的应用。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in biomedical and cognitive neuroscience, but their ability to reason reliably over heterogeneous knowledge graphs (KGs) remains unclear. We benchmark five LLMs (GPT-5.1, Gemini 2.0 Pro, Llama 3, Qwen, and BioGPT) across five KGs: DrugBank, NeuroSynth, Cognitive Atlas, Brain Map, and GDPKG. We introduce a multitier query dataset to evaluate reasoning depth and factual accuracy under KG-aware (graph-grounded) and KG-agnostic conditions. Inter-annotator agreement was substantial, with Cohen's kappa of 0.781 (range: 0.753-0.807) and Fleiss' kappa of 0.77. Results show that general-purpose models, particularly GPT-5.1 and Gemini 2.0 Pro, generally outperform domain-specific models on multi-hop and high-level reasoning tasks, although performance declines as query complexity increases. KG grounding reduces hallucination rates across all evaluated models. Contrary to our initial hypothesis, domain-specific models do not consistently outperform general-purpose models on specialized tasks without graph grounding. These findings highlight the value of combining broad language-model capabilities with structured domain knowledge. We provide practical guidance for selecting LLM-KG integration strategies based on task complexity and computational constraints, and discuss hybrid neurosymbolic architectures for personalized medicine, cognitive disorder diagnosis, and explainable AI.

</details>


### [235] [Learning the Task or Learning the Verifier? Rethinking Criteria Exposure in Embodied Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08318)
*Wenbo Huang,Jianxin Bi,Shunyu Yang,Ruiyang Si,Rui Ni,Qiang Wang,Shulong Jiang,Yintianrun Zhang,Feixiang Ruan,Kewei Zhang,Ye Huang,Shukai Gong,Daquan Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究对比了机器人模拟基准中的自适应代码生成代理，在暴露于基准标准的情况下，其代码的表现不如未暴露于标准的版本。研究进一步提出了一种忠实于指令的标准，并证明它能更好地反映代理生成程序的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自适应编码代理在机器人模拟基准上的表现逐渐提高，本文研究了这些代理在暴露于基准标准的条件下表现出来的代码是否能够更好地完成指令任务，还是仅仅是过度拟合验证标准。因此，研究了暴露于基准标准的代理和未暴露的标准的代理的表现。

Method: 作者进行了一系列受控实验，构建了一个自适应改善的代理，并使用和未使用标准暴露方式对代理进行训练，然后对比了两种情况下的结果。

Result: 在暴露于标准的情况下，代理生成的代码在严格的标准下表现得更好，但当标准略有变化时不适用于原始指令的任务。进一步提出的忠实于指令的标准能够更好地反映代理生成程序的性能。

Conclusion: 该研究证明，在基准标准暴露的情况下，编码代理可能仅仅是为了通过验证标准而产生代码，而不是更好地完成指令。因此建议使用忠实于指令的标准来更好地评估代理的性能。

Abstract: Self-improving embodied agents are increasingly being developed and compared on robot simulation benchmarks. Several recent works expose the benchmark's success criteria to the learning agent, a practice we call Criteria Exposure (CE). These criteria, originally written to verify policies trained by imitation learning, often demand more than the corresponding language instructions, so a score from such a verifier may not faithfully reflect whether the instructed task was completed, especially for robot-control programs written by an agent. As embodied coding agents report ever higher benchmark scores, a natural question arises: when a coding agent is exposed to the benchmark's criteria, does it generate code that better performs the instructed task, or code that simply overfits to the verification criteria? We conduct controlled experiments with a self-improving agent with and without CE, and find that with CE the agent quickly produces code with a higher success rate under the given criteria, but this advantage disappears when the criteria are changed slightly without departing from the original instructions. More importantly, the skills the agent develops under CE transfer poorly across tasks. We further propose instruction-faithful criteria and show that they better reflect the performance of agent-generated programs.

</details>


### [236] [Enhancing Local and Global Structure in Compressed AI Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08341)
*Henry Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对大规模文本嵌入向量的压缩问题，提出了一种称为局部感知量化（LAQ）的方法，通过优化代码本以提高全局保真度，实现了在减少文本压缩量的同时保持文本主题的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有压缩方法在压缩大量文本嵌入向量时，虽然能够在保存近邻信息的基础上保持局部保真度，但难以兼顾全局保真度，尤其是文本主题一致性的问题。因此，针对这一挑战，提出了一种基于局部距离感知的量化方法以改善压缩效果。

Method: 本文提出了基于局部距离感知的量化（LAQ）方法，该方法通过首先使用重新评分的二进制代码和PCA方法评估不同压缩策略下的保真度，然后通过证明一个用于整体保真度的上下界，并将其转化为一个针对OPQ索引代码本的边缘目标函数来优化存储代码，从而改善压缩效果。

Result: 通过大量实验，结果证明在96字节/段落的情况下，重新评分的二进制代码可以达到局部保真度0.97，同时10.1%的段落转移到另一个主题；相比之下，通过PCA方法使用8位代码，局部保真度为0.77，但仅有1.7%的段落被重新分配。LAQ方法在所有实验中都能够有效提升整体保真度，且相比于其他相同大小的量化方法表现最佳。

Conclusion: 本文提出的LAQ方法有效地解决了大规模文本嵌入向量压缩问题中的局部和全局保真度之间的矛盾，不仅能够保持局部一致性的最邻近距离信息，同时还能提高整体主题的一致性，具有很高的实际应用价值。

Abstract: AI agents store text as embedding vectors and compress them at scale. A compression is accepted when each item's nearest neighbors survive, but agents also consolidate, delete, and compare memories, tasks that depend on which items lie far apart. We evaluate both ends with two label-free fidelities from our prior work: the local fidelity $L$ at 10 neighbors and the global fidelity $G$ at 30 to 50 percent of the memory. At 96 bytes per passage, rescored binary codes reach an $L$ of 0.97 yet move 10.1 percent of passages to another theme; PCA with 8-bit codes, at an $L$ of 0.77, moves 1.7 percent. Rescoring leaves the stored codes, and thus $G$, unchanged. We prove that across the $G$ band, $1-ν_k$ is at most 2 to 2.18 times the square root of the near-far rank-violation rate, and that this rate follows the distance noise of the compression (correlation 0.979). Our locality-aware quantization (LAQ) turns the bound into a hinge objective on the codebooks of an OPQ index, keeping its bytes, rotation, and search. To our knowledge, LAQ is the first quantizer whose objective comes from a proven bound on the far end. Reconstruction alone raises $G$ in 0 of 52 held-out runs; the far hinge raises it in all 52. LAQ raises $G$ in all 52 ($p=8.5\times 10^{-4}$ over 13 memory-encoder groups), including the external FinDER memory, has the highest $G$ of seven equal-size quantizers in all 12 Multi-Doc-2025 runs, scales to a million passages, and keeps evidence recall.

</details>


### [237] [NPCBench: A Clinical Apprenticeship Benchmark for Multimodal Care-Pathway Reasoning in Nasopharyngeal Carcinoma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08369)
*Pengkai Wang,Wei-Wei Zhang,Yan Li,Min Tang,Zhitian Hou,Zeyu Liu,Guanghao Zhu,Yuanyi Wang,Yanggan Gu,Wenjun Wang,Minheng Ni,Congkai Xie,Zhijie Sang,Jianmin Wu,Ying Sun,Hongxia Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NPCBench 提供了一个鼻咽癌（NPC）的临床学徒基准，涵盖了从指南和基础知识到案例规划、长期复查、CarePath 整合和开放式咨询的全面训练，包含了4,777个分组件和咨询项目，以及一个包含100个患者的CarePath队列。这个基准测试了模型是否可以综合专业知识，追踪患者状态，并在医疗咨询中做出合理决定。研究观察到，单独任务的优秀表现并不能保证在同一患者上的诊断和护理答案的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的医学基准通常只涉及孤立的事实问题、单图像任务或静态案例，但没有充分考察模型能否将亚专科知识与多模态证据结合，并在整个治疗过程中持续跟踪患者的病情。NPCBench 的引入旨在填补这一空白，提供一种更全面的临床技能评估基准。

Method: NPCBench 包含4777个组件项目和咨询服务项，以及包含100名患者和417项评分诊断和护理任务的CarePath队列。评价体系涵盖了从指南和基础知识到案例规划、长期复查、CarePath整合和开放式咨询的各个步骤。研究人员评估了30个模型的表现，并分析了多模态模型在严格CarePath成功上的局限。

Result: 研究发现，在30个评估的模型中，单一任务的优秀性能未能保证同一患者的诊断和护理答案在CarePath中的联合正确性。21个多模态模型中，严格CarePath成功的最高比例仅为23%。进一步分析显示，模型在证据基础、长期修订和咨询评估中的表现存在差距。

Conclusion: NPCBench 不仅仅局限于静态问答，而是通过模拟临床学习过程，提供了一个基于证据和时间连贯性的推理测试平台，适用于肿瘤学的临床工作流程评估。

Abstract: Current medical benchmarks typically break down clinical competence into isolated factual questions, single-image tasks, or static case vignettes. These tests score individual answers, but reveal less about whether a model can integrate subspecialty knowledge with multimodal evidence and track a patient's condition throughout a course of care. To close this gap, we introduce NPCBench, a clinical apprenticeship benchmark for nasopharyngeal carcinoma (NPC). NPCBench mirrors specialist training, from guideline and foundational knowledge to case planning, longitudinal review, CarePath integration, and open-ended consultation. It contains 4,777 component and consultation items and a 100-patient CarePath cohort with 417 scored diagnostic and care tasks. The episode-level evaluation tests whether diagnostic and care answers are jointly correct within a care pathway. By linking stepwise tasks to patient-level care trajectories, NPCBench evaluates whether models ground decisions in visual evidence, interpret changes in patient state, and meet clinical requirements in consultation. Across 30 evaluated models, we observe a composition gap: strong performance on individual tasks does not ensure jointly correct diagnostic and care answers for the same patient. Among 21 multimodal models, strict CarePath success reaches at most 23%. Paired controls and physician calibration further examine evidence grounding, longitudinal revision, and consultation judging. Overall, NPCBench broadens the evaluation of medical artificial intelligence beyond static question-answering paradigms to an apprenticeship-oriented assessment of clinical workflows, thereby offering a testbed for evidence-based and temporally coherent reasoning in oncology care.

</details>


### [238] [Learning a patient world model for health system simulation in intensive care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08392)
*Yingtai Li,Ruoyan Wang,Yu Li,Shaohua Kevin Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过学习长期电子健康记录（EHR）中的患者动力学模型，提出了一种能够适用于多个场景的患者世界模型。该模型通过神经网络从随时间变化的患者状态中学习，并在重症监护病房（ICU）操作情景中得到了验证，表明此模型能够准确模拟患者的护理需求、任务和出院建议，优于传统基于状态的预测模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 健康系统的模拟至关重要，但传统方法受限于单一政策问题的自定义状态转换，难以应对新问题带来的不同临床信息，需要重新定义状态和转换，效率低下。因此，研究旨在通过学习患者的历史数据来创建一个通用的动力学模型，解决这一问题。

Method: 该研究采用了神经网络从纵向电子健康记录中学习患者动力学模型。首先，构建了一个基于神经网络的患者状态模型，该模型可以从随时间变化的患者数据中学习。然后，在Intensive Care Unit (ICU)操作情境中训练此模型，并将其时间可查询的预测结果耦合到特定场景下可用于ICU运营的日历、资源和队列之中。

Result: 实验表明，该动力学模型在ICU容量、护理调度以及ICU后转出等方面的表现优于马尔可夫和半马尔可夫模型。对于从不同先前路径进入同一支持状态的患者，该模型捕捉到了24小时内恢复率（-6.4%预测值和-6.7%观察值）之间的差异，这是紧凑状态模型未能捕捉到的。此外，该模型能够准确模拟随时间变化的患者护理需求、队列负担和床位使用。

Conclusion: 该研究指出，学习到的患者动力学模型有潜力替代问题特定状态模型，通过重新配置场景而不是重建模型，即可对新的护理政策进行评价，提高了研究效率和准确性。

Abstract: Designing healthcare strategies requires anticipating their clinical and operational consequences before implementation, a task that often relies on health system simulation. Such a simulation necessitates modeling how patient states evolve and generate care demands. However, conventional approaches typically tailor compact state transitions to a single policy question and omit history-dependent dynamics, thus requiring the redefinition of states and transitions when there is a new question that entails different clinical information. We propose to learn a patient world model from longitudinal EHRs using a neural network to provide a shared clinical representation of patient trajectories that can serve simulation questions across scenarios. Taking ICU operations as an example, we train the model on MIMIC-IV, freeze it, and couple its time-queryable predictions to shared ICU calendars, resources, and queues via scenario-specific mappings, so that one model renders admission demand, care tasks, and discharge proposals across simulations evaluating ICU bed capacity, care dispatch, and post-ICU transfer capacity. Experiments show that our model matches Markov and semi-Markov comparators in aggregate occupancy and directional responses to capacity and dispatch policies. It also reproduces policy gains from replayed observed trajectories. For patients entering the same support state from different prior trajectories, the learned representation captures a 24-h recovery difference (-6.4 predicted versus -6.7 observed percentage points), a contrast missed by compact state models, which propagates into distinct simulated care demand, queue burden, and bed use. These results indicate that a learned world model of patient dynamics has the potential to replace question-specific state models, allowing new care policies to be evaluated by reconfiguring the scenario without model rebuilding.

</details>


### [239] [StepBack: Step-Level Error-Localized Resampling for Efficient Test-Time Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08403)
*Yang Ouyang,Jung-Eun Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: StepBack 是一种新的解码策略，它通过局部化错误位置并在出错步骤及其后续步骤重新采样来提高效率，从而减少了对完整推理轨迹的采样次数。与传统的自一致性方法相比，它可以减少高达41.26%的令牌使用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自一致性方法在提高链式推理方面的效果显著，但需要大量的采样轨迹，计算成本较高。StepBack 研究旨在通过局部化错误来改进这些方法，从而提供一种更高效的方式来实现相同水平的推理准确度。

Method: StepBack 方法的关键在于通过轻量级信号局部化错误位置，然后只在错误步骤及其后续步骤重新采样，最后通过多数投票确定答案。

Result: 在七项数学推理和多跳 QA 基准测试中，StepBack 的准确率与传统的自一致性方法相当，但在令牌使用量上减少了高达41.26%。

Conclusion: StepBack 方法为自一致性解码策略提供了更高的效率，尤其是对于资源受限的环境特别有利，并且可以与自适应推理方法结合使用，增加其在推理时间的效率。

Abstract: Self-consistency (SC) is a widely used decoding strategy for improving chain-of-thought reasoning, but it requires sampling many complete reasoning trajectories with a preset budget. Its efficient variants reduce the cost mainly by stopping full-trajectory sampling early or distributing the number of samples across questions. In this paper, we propose StepBack, a step-level error-localized resampling method for efficient test-time reasoning. Given a small set of draft trajectories, StepBack localizes likely first error positions using lightweight signals and then resamples the detected error step and its suffix, followed by majority answer voting. Empirically, StepBack matches the accuracy of SC on average across seven mathematical reasoning and multi-hop QA benchmarks, while reducing token usage by up to 41.26%. In addition, by nature, StepBack can be combined with adaptive inference methods such as Adaptive Consistency, further improving their inference-time efficiency.

</details>


### [240] [The Gold Rush in AI4Math, II: What are signatures of AI-assisted Proofs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08412)
*Yuanchuan Guo,Jiashun Jin,Zheng Tracy Ke,Bingcheng Sui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了检测证明中的人工智能参与程度，通过构建五个包含8281篇论文的集合，并引入ProofTrace框架来表征和提取具有可解释性的数学组织特征，揭示了人工智能参与的逐步变化，以及ProofTrace在区分人类和AI撰写的证明方面的优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能在数学中的应用增多，研究者们开始考虑如何独立于人工智能辅助写作的情况下检测证明中是否存在人工智能的参与。

Method: 作者构建了五个包含不同层度AI参与的论文组，使用ProofTrace框架将每篇论文表示为一个包含即时证明支持（GIPS）的图，从而提取具有标识AI参与的可解释性特征。

Result: 研究结果表明，使用主要成分投影，可以观察到一种AI参与程度的定性梯度；发现AI生成证明的一些重叠模式，包括较少引用外部结果、更大的依赖于中间结论以及图连接的变化；ProofTrace在区分人类和人工智能撰写证明方面表现优于最先进的文本检测器，即使经过人工智能重写也能保持稳定。

Conclusion: 本文识别出了一些新的迹象，表明人工智能生成的证明，并且证明了ProofTrace在检测其差异性方面的有效性。

Abstract: The growing use of AI in mathematics raises a question: can we detect AI involvement in proofs, separately from AI assistance with writing? Historical arXiv papers, recent papers disclosing substantive AI use, and manuscripts released by OpenAI provide a rich empirical basis for studying this question. However, several challenges remain: lack of matched case-control pairs; potential incompleteness of disclosure; and lack of ready-to-use features designed to capture signals of AI involvement in proofs.
  To address these challenges, we construct five cohorts comprising 8,281 papers by combining existing manuscripts with those we produced using generative AI. These cohorts span different levels of AI involvement and include matched pairs of papers addressing the same research problems. We then introduce ProofTrace, a framework that represents each paper as a graph of immediate proof support (GIPS) and extracts interpretable features of mathematical organization.
  Our study yields three discoveries. First, principal-component projections of our features reveal a qualitative gradient of AI involvement, where AI-reworded papers remain close to human papers, the two AI-generated cohorts depart in broadly shared directions, and papers disclosing substantive AI use occupy intermediate positions which shifts toward AI-generated cohorts over time. Second, we identify recurring signatures of AI-generated proofs, such as fewer externally cited results, greater reliance on intermediate conclusions, and altered graph connectivity; many of these signatures persist under controls for research field, manuscript length, or graph size. Finally, in a separate detection experiment, ProofTrace distinguishes human-written papers from those disclosing substantive AI use. Its performance remains stable under AI rewording, whereas the performance of state-of-the-art text-based detectors declines sharply.

</details>


### [241] [Benchmarks as Software: Terminal-Bench and the Case for Continuous Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08417)
*E. Kelly Buchanan,Crystal Zhou,Scott W. Linderman,Christopher Ré*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个连续验证的工作流来维护基准测试，通过社区报告、任务工件、代理跟踪、自动化判例检查和人工审核修复了Terminal-Bench 2.0的缺陷，并展示了资源分配对代理性能影响显著，从而揭示了基准测试维护债务的复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准评估可能变得陈旧，随着模型的提高，可能会发现现有验证器拒绝的有效解决方案，或者评估基础设施的变化会否决任务和测试中的假设。因此，本文提出了一种持续验证工作流来解决基准维护债务问题。

Method: 该连续验证工作流结合了社区报告、任务工件、代理跟踪、自动化判例检查和人工审核修复，应用此工作流于Terminal-Bench 2.0，进行审计识别问题并修复。展示了通过延长时间限制提高模型表现等案例。

Result: 对该基准测试的审计结果表明，大约30%的基准存在缺陷，这些修复增加了准确度超过10个百分点，排名变动最多达三名。同时，展示了固定时间限制对模型性能的重要影响。整体排名与修订后的排名仍然高度相关(Spearman rho = 0.958)。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了随着模型性能提升，评估假设和资源设置可能需要重新验证，以解决基准维护债务问题，同时强调了资源分配在评估指标定义中的重要性。

Abstract: Coding agents are among the most economically consequential deployments of LLMs to date, making reliable evaluation increasingly important. Yet even benchmarks that are carefully validated at release can become stale. As models improve, they can discover valid solutions that existing verifiers reject, while changes to evaluation infrastructure can invalidate assumptions embedded in tasks and tests. We call the resulting accumulation of defects benchmark maintenance debt: a growing gap between benchmark scores and the capabilities they are intended to measure. We introduce a continuous-validation workflow for agentic benchmarks that combines community reports, task artifacts, agent traces, automated oracle checks, and human-gated repairs. We apply this workflow to Terminal-Bench 2.0, a benchmark that underwent extensive human and LM-assisted review and red-teaming before release. Our audit identifies issues in roughly 30% of the benchmark, and we release the resulting repairs as Terminal-Bench 2.1. These repairs change evaluation results: accuracy increased by over 10 percentage points and leaderboard positions move by up to three ranks, even as the overall ranking remains highly correlated (Spearman rho = 0.958).
  We evaluate how resource allocation shapes benchmark conclusions. We show that fixed wall-clock can materially change agent performance: For example, extending the time limit closes a 20pp harness gap on GPT 5.4 and shrinks a 30 pp gap on GPT 5.4 mini to 5 pp. Benchmark maintenance is therefore not only about repairing task artifacts: resource budgets are part of the measurement specification and must be validated alongside the tasks themselves.
  More broadly, benchmark maintenance debt is not just accumulated implementation bugs: our results show that as models improve, both evaluation assumptions and resource settings may need to be revalidated.

</details>


### [242] [The Power of Meta Access in the Software of the Future --- Demonstrated on LLM Chat UIs and Chat AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08428)
*Julian Kunkel,Ali Doosthosseini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了LLM助手通过Meta Access获得对界面的结构化和权限受限的访问，提出了一种层次结构，从功能辅导到全面编排，并展示了如何使聊天成为整个工作区的控制层，而不牺牲用户控制权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的聊天界面仅能看到当前的对话，无法看到和操作周围的环境，这限制了LLM助手的能力。因此，作者探索了通过Meta Access赋予助手对界面的结构化视图，以提升其功能。

Method: 作者提出了一个分层的层次结构，并展示了如何在Meta Chat原型中实现这一设想。

Result: Meta Chat原型展示了如何通过暴露应用程序状态来使聊天成为整个工作区的控制层，扩展了聊天功能而不过度干预用户控制。

Conclusion: 随着助手嵌入到IDE、仪表板、办公套件和操作系统中，AI时代的界面应暴露其结构化和权限受限的状态和能力模型。

Abstract: Chat remains the default surface for many large language model (LLM) tasks, even as agentic tools such as OpenCode excel at coding and automation. Yet the model sees the current dialogue but not the workspace around it. We explore meta access---giving the assistant a structured, permissioned view of the interface itself and propose a ladder of levels: feature tutoring; metadata, settings, and preference access; retrieval over summaries and messages, confirmed mutation, and full orchestration. Meta access can turn a chat client from many screens into one entry point for finding, navigating, organising, and operating it, while raising risks around grounding, privacy, prompt injection, and trust. General-purpose agentic harnesses are runtimes; because they can change their own settings, meta access becomes reflexive and needs a separate control-plane boundary. We ground this in the Meta Chat prototype of the Chat AI client. It builds bounded context from, e.g., summaries, settings, and relevant messages; links references to conversations, topics, and personas; supports confirmed, reversible changes; offers a browsable tutorial library; and recommends personas as user-started conversations. By exposing application state in structured form, meta access can extend screen readers from widget descriptions to semantic workspace operations. Meta access thus turns chat from a window onto one conversation into a control layer for the whole workspace, extending chat toward agentic capability without surrendering user control. As assistants become embedded in IDEs, dashboards, office suites, and operating systems, AI-era interfaces should expose a structured, permissioned model of their state and capabilities, ideally by opening their existing metamodels through a debugger-like interface.

</details>


### [243] [ReproBridge: Building Reproducibility into ML Research Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08452)
*Yubo Wang,Rui Meng,Jaehyun Nam,Tomas Pfister,Jinsung Yoon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过标准化的可重现制品设计和引入ReproBridge建立流程，改进了机器学习实验的独立再现成功率，并降低了后续API成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器学习实验再现涉及跨论文、公开代码和支持附件的信息拼凑，这依赖于重现编码代理去填补缺失的细节，而不是仅依赖公开的制品。为了改善这一过程，作者设计了一套标准化的制品模型，并引入了ReproBridge代理工作流来构建这些改进的、可再现的制品。

Method: 作者定义了一个标准化的制品设计，包括细化代码库、环境设置、实验启动器和再现指南，以及开发了一个名为ReproBridge的代理工作流，用于构建这些细化的、可重现的制品。此外，他们还提供了ReproBridge-Bench数据集，包括来自36篇论文的387项实验的1,029个任务和五个人工验证的参考得分。

Result: 实验涉及四种模型，使用细化的制品比仅使用原始代码的再现成功率提高了16.2%，平均API成本降低了19.7%。所有模型的平均成功率达到90%以上，且差异从21.6%缩减到3.9%。移除任何一部分都会显著降低成功率，最大的损失来自代码和环境的移除。这表明保留执行知识即使对于低原始代码成功率的模型也能提高再现实验的可靠性，并降低API成本。

Conclusion: 这项研究表明，保留执行知识可以帮助即使对于低原始代码成功率的模型也能够更可靠地再现实验，降低API成本。标准化的可重现制品设计和ReproBridge代理工作流的提出为提高实验再现的可靠性和效率提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Reproducing machine learning experiments requires reconstructing procedures from information scattered across papers, released code, and supporting artifacts. For coding agents, reproduction success can therefore depend on their ability to fill in missing details, rather than on the released artifacts alone. We ask whether preserving recovered procedures and decisions improves subsequent independent reproduction. We define a standardized artifact design comprising a refined codebase, environment setup, experiment launchers, and a reproduction guide, and introduce ReproBridge, an agent workflow for constructing these refined reproducible artifacts. ReproBridge-Bench provides 1,029 tasks from 387 experiments across 36 papers, anchored by five human-verified reference scores per task. Agents attempt tasks in fresh sessions with raw code or refined artifacts; a separate Auditor checks execution evidence. Across four evaluated models, refined artifacts raise mean reproduction success from 76.4% to 92.6% and reduce downstream API spending by 19.7% on average. The cross-model success-rate spread narrows from 21.6 to 3.9 percentage points, with all four models achieving mean success rates above 90%. Removing any component reduces success in the ablation study, with the largest losses from code and environment removal. These results suggest that preserving execution knowledge helps even models with lower raw-code success reproduce experiments more reliably and at lower API cost.

</details>


### [244] [Scientist World: Grow a Research World for AI-Scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08478)
*Taiming Lu,Sachin Konan,Zhuang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究构建了一个名为Scientist World的平台，它将科研论文转化为可执行环境，让AI科学家能够评估自己的能力。研究结果显示，前沿的AI科学家有74%的时间能够超越人类研究人员。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI科学家的出现，有必要创造一个能够让AI科学家开展研究的环境，使其能够利用人类科学家积累多年的开放问题和共享理念，通过实现每个论文中的开放性研究问题和方法，来评估AI科学家的能力。

Method: 研究团队开发了一个自主的管道，从每一项研究论文中提取开放性研究问题，并重现已提出的实验方法作为基准。研究人员还在环境中引入了AI科学家，并评估他们的表现。

Result: 研究团队发布了1000个经过验证的环境，结果显示，前沿的AI科学家能够74%的时间超过人类研究人员。此外，这些环境还记录了6400小时的科研流程，包含了所有的想法、实验、失败和成功。

Conclusion: 作为一个记录AI科学家如何进行科学研究的平台，Scientist World不仅展示了AI在科学研究方面的潜力，还提供了一个训练场所，以提高AI科学家的研究思维能力。

Abstract: AI-Scientists arrived, but a world for them to grow in did not. Human scientists take theirs for granted $-$ centuries of open problems and shared ideas, waiting in the scientific literature. To AI-Scientists, that world is readable but not runnable, so we build Scientist World for them: every research paper becomes an executable environment. Our autonomous pipeline extracts each paper's open-ended research question and reproduces its proposed method as the baseline; an AI-Scientist entering the environment is evaluated against what the human authors achieved. We release the first 1,000 verified environments. With the world as an evaluation, we found that frontier AI-Scientists can already surpass the human researchers 74% of the time. As a record of how AI does science, it contains 6,400 hours of research traces, with every idea, experiment, failure, and success. As a training ground, we are able to raise (+158%) a small model to match frontier models in research ideation. At last, AI-Scientists' own world has arrived, grown from and growing with science itself.

</details>


### [245] [SwarmEvolve: Latent Agent Swarms for Evolutionary Discovery and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08483)
*Qile Jiang,George Em Karniadakis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SwarmEvolve是一种框架，它采用 hundreds of 低成本代理，并在 swarms 中进行 reasoning，通过在 latent space 传递 KV 缓存进行信息共享，使用了 novelty filter 来保留最具差异性的代理思想，并在 sumamrizer agent 中实现了想法的自然 crossover。该框架在 21 个数学发现和科学计算问题上表现最佳，达到顶级性能所需时间仅为其他先进框架的五分之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM-guided 计算和数学问题求解过程往往非常缓慢，因为需要多次生成长文本的思路链来维持种群多样性。而 SwarmEvolve 试图通过使用低成本代理在 latent space 中共享信息并直接比较隐藏状态向量的相似度来提高效率。

Method: SwarmEvolve 通过构建多个代理组成的 swarms 来协同工作，每个代理从不同角度研究问题，通过直接操作隐藏状态向量并传递 KV 缓存实现信息共享，同时使用 novelty filter 来净化想法，利用一个 aggregator 去总结 swarms 中所有成员的 KV 缓存进行创意 crossover。

Result: 在 21 个数学和科学计算问题上，SwarmEvolve 平均得分最高，并且在达到顶级性能所需时间方面有着显著优势，通常只需要其他框架的一半甚至更短的时间就能达到。

Conclusion: SwarmEvolve 显示了在种群搜索中使用基于 latent space 的代理通信方式可高效低成本地解决科学问题。

Abstract: LLM-guided evolutionary search uses AI agents to improve programs for mathematical and computational problems by iteratively refining solutions and combining high-scoring methods. However, the process is often slow because the LLM generates a long textual chain of thought for each round and must repeat this generation many times to maintain population diversity. We present SwarmEvolve, a framework in which hundreds of low-cost agents per round work in swarms, reason in latent space without decoding to text, and share information by passing KV caches. Each swarm studies the problem from a different angle. A novelty filter keeps the most distinct agent ideas by directly measuring the distances between the agents' hidden state vectors. Crossover of ideas happens naturally in latent space, as a summarizer agent receives the combined KV caches of all swarm members. On 21 problems in mathematical discovery and scientific computing, SwarmEvolve gets the highest average score and needs a median of only 3 to 8 minutes to reach the best scores that other state-of-the-art frameworks reach in 60 minutes. On several problems, SwarmEvolve with Qwen3 8B improves on the solutions that AlphaEvolve first found with frontier LLMs. Our work shows that agent swarms that communicate in latent space offer a fast, low-cost approach to program search for scientific problems.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [246] [Rule-Guided and Trajectory-Aware Repair for LLM-Based HLS Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07324)
*Xianjing Zhu,Wenqi Lou,Yuanbo Wen,Teng Wang,Lei Gong,Chao Wang,Xuehai Zhou*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: MementoHLS 提出了一种基于可执行和可反驳的双内存引导修复的 HLS 生成框架，通过 Executable Repair Contract Library (ERCL) 和 Reviewer-Grounded Falsification Ledger (RFL) 子系统来改进多轮工具反馈和重用 HLS 知识的过程，从而提高了 HLS 生成的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 HLS 生成方法在处理多轮工具反馈和重用 HLS 知识时常常表现不佳，鉴于此，研究团队开发了 MementoHLS 以提升 HLS 生成过程的有效性。

Method: MementoHLS 框架包括 Executable Repair Contract Library (ERCL) 和 Reviewer-Grounded Falsification Ledger (RFL) 两个模块，ERCL 将跨任务的 HLS 知识转化为带证据的修复行动，RFL 将任务本地的修复历史转化为轨迹证据来抑制无效路径，同时保留验证过的进展。此外，通过使用 Same-Candidate Reviewer 和 Single-Commit Arbiter，确保了产出责任的可靠归属。

Result: MementoHLS 在 HLS-Eval 和 Bench4HLS 任务上的评估结果显示，其在 HLS 生成可靠性方面有显著提升。对于 HLS-Eval，改进后的 TB 和 Synth pass@1 为 58.9%，pass@5 为 68.1%，而 Bench4HLS 则对应地从 15.5% 和 32.9% 提高到了 32.4% 和 50.6%。

Conclusion: 总体而言，MementoHLS 框架展示了 HLS 生成过程中对多循环工具反馈和重用 HLS 知识的改进，能够显著提升 HLS 生成的可靠性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) offer a promising path for directly generating functionally correct and synthesizable HLS-C from natural-language specifications. However, existing approaches often struggle to turn multi-round tool feedback and reusable HLS knowledge into effective revisions, limiting end-to-end generation success. We propose MementoHLS, an agentic HLS generation framework built around executable and falsifiable dual-memory-guided repair. MementoHLS combines an Executable Repair Contract Library (ERCL), which converts cross-task HLS knowledge into evidence-gated repair actions, with a Reviewer-Grounded Falsification Ledger (RFL), which converts task-local repair history into trajectory evidence to suppress ineffective routes and preserve validated progress. To ensure reliable outcome attribution, a Same-Candidate Reviewer binds Parse, Compile, Testbench (TB), and Synthesis (Synth) outcomes to the corresponding candidate, while a Single-Commit Arbiter commits at most one candidate per round. Evaluated on 94 HLS-Eval and 170 Bench4HLS tasks, MementoHLS improves same-candidate TB$\wedge$Synth pass@1/pass@5 from 41.7%/57.4% to 58.9%/68.1% on HLS-Eval, and from 15.5%/32.9% to 32.4%/50.6% on Bench4HLS, demonstrating substantial improvements in end-to-end HLS generation reliability.

</details>


### [247] [A Synchronized Tri-Modality Wireless System for Multi-Point Non-Contact Cardiac Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07358)
*Wei Ren,Kaili Zhao,Huansheng Yi,Miaoyang Hu,Jianqi Wang,Canhua Xu,Fugui Qi*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种三模态无接触雷达心脏监测系统，结合了ECG、SCG和DCG，实现了精确的模态间同步和可穿戴操作，验证了雷达在监测心脏方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的心电图(EGG)和心血管组织检测技术存在局限，无法提供直接的机械校准标准。该研究旨在通过整合多种生理信号实现精确的心脏监测，填补了这一空白。

Method: 该系统集成了微型可穿戴的ECG/SCG模块和高分辨率毫米波雷达模块，实现了时间和空间上的同步记录与分析。

Result: 系统在移动平台上验证了DCG和SCG的位移测量一致性，并在人体试验中展示了模态间的高一致性，以及雷达输出的关键特征点的提取能力，其中ECG作为电学参考，SCG作为机械基准。

Conclusion: 该系统为非接触心脏传感提供了一个严格的空间解析验证平台，推动了下一代雷达基监测技术的发展。

Abstract: Cardiac physiological monitoring is fundamental to evaluating cardiovascular health and diagnosing related diseases. Non-contact radar sensing can capture chest-wall vibrations with spatial specificity, enabling localized physiological monitoring and cardiac assessment. Progress, however, has been limited by the absence of a reliable, research-grade hardware platform for rigorous validation of radar-based methods. Prior systems typically pair radar, namely Doppler cardiography (DCG), with only electrocardiography (ECG) or other coarse references, providing no direct mechanical ground truth and thus constraining fine-grained evaluation of radar outputs. We present a tri-modality wireless system that integrates ECG, seismocardiography (SCG), and DCG under a unified acquisition and control framework for time-aligned recording and analysis. The tri-modality system combines miniaturized wearable ECG/SCG modules with a high-resolution mmWave radar module, supporting spatially resolved sensing and subsequent feature extraction. To our knowledge, this is the first integrated ECG--SCG--DCG hardware system for comprehensive cardiac monitoring; it enables multi-point thoracic acquisition, achieves precise cross-modal synchronization, and maintains specifications suitable for wearable operation. Experiments on a linear-motion platform show close agreement between DCG- and SCG-derived displacements, confirming accurate displacement measurement. Human-subject studies further demonstrate cross-modal consistency and robust extraction of feature points, establishing ECG as the electrical anchor and SCG as the mechanical ground truth for DCG. Overall, the system provides a rigorous, spatially resolved validation platform that advances non-contact cardiac sensing and supports next-generation radar-based monitoring.

</details>


### [248] [OpenRDMA: A GPU-Native Multi-Path Reliable RDMA Transport Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07559)
*Ziheng Ji,Chunyu Liu,Weihao Peng,Mingheng Mi,Pu Wang,Hai Zhang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为OpenRDMA的开源多路径可靠RDMA传输架构，旨在为GPU友好的通信提供低开销请求提交、多路径传输及有序DMA放置等功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型AI集群计算密度的提升，网络通信成为系统性能瓶颈。传统GPUDirect RDMA和InfiniBand GPUDirect Async需要CPU或GPU承担额外开销，从而影响性能。因此，需要一种既能降低请求提交开销、又能支持多路径传输及可靠数据传输的RDMA架构。

Method: OpenRDMA通过一系列创新技术实现其目标。首先，它提供了一个GPU门铃机制，允许GPU发出8字节令牌并由专门的FPGA网卡重建WQE。其次，OpenRDMA利用多路径可靠连接技术，支持按包的多路径传输和乱序到达，并通过选择性重传、有序DMA放置和GPU可见标志轮询保障可靠传输和安全数据消费。最后，它的重传机制由硬件和软件共同设计，使GPU提交路径保持轻量，并降低FPGA硬件状态开销。

Result: 此研究通过有效的技术设计降低了GPU通信的开销，改进了多路径传输和可靠数据传输，并提供了FPGA原型实现。具体而言，OpenRDMA为GPU友好的通信提供了低延时的请求提交方式和对复杂数据传输需求的支持。

Conclusion: 未来的工作将涉及OpenRDMA的完全实现和性能测试，以验证其在实际场景中的可行性和性能提升。

Abstract: As the computing power density of large-scale AI clusters rises, network communication increasingly limits system performance. GPU-initiated communication requires low-overhead request submission to overlap communication with computation. However, GPUDirect RDMA (GDR) still relies on CPU involvement in its control path, while InfiniBand GPUDirect Async (IBGDA) requires GPUs to assemble Work Queue Entries (WQEs), consuming additional SM resources and memory bandwidth. We propose OpenRDMA, an open-source multi-path reliable RDMA transport architecture designed for GPU-friendly communication. First, OpenRDMA provides a minimal GPU doorbell mechanism: the GPU issues an 8-byte token, and a dedicated FPGA NIC reconstructs the complete WQE from preconfigured context. Second, building on Multipath Reliable Connection (MRC), OpenRDMA supports per-packet multi-path transport and out-of-order arrivals, while selective retransmission, ordered DMA placement, and GPU-visible flag polling provide reliable delivery and safe data consumption. Third, a hardware-software co-designed retransmission mechanism keeps the GPU submission path lightweight and reduces FPGA hardware state overhead: the NIC handles fast data transfer, while CPU software manages complex timeout detection, state tracking, and recovery policies. We describe the architecture and key hardware components of OpenRDMA; implementation of the FPGA prototype is underway.

</details>


### [249] [CAMP: Context-Aware Mapping and Placement for LLMs on 3D-IMC Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07707)
*Orhun Görkem,Athanasios Vasilopoulos,Georgios Pagonis,Victoria Clerico,Hadjer Benmeziane,Irem Boybat,Hsinyu Tsai,Abu Sebastian,William Andrew Simon*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: CAMP是一种针对3D IMC加速器的上下文感知权重映射算法，它通过冲突矩阵和监控驱动的反馈循环优化计算资源利用，从而显著提高了吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D-IMC架构在权重映射方面存在不足，导致计算资源利用率低下。CAMP旨在解决这一问题，通过考虑架构、目标网络和运行时上下文来优化权重映射。

Method: CAMP通过构建冲突矩阵，并利用监视驱动的反馈循环来优化权重映射。它还能有效缩减搜索空间，确保在实用运行时间内得出最佳映射。

Result: CAMP相较于现有最优映射算法实现了3D-IMC计算利用率从42.4%提升到60.3%，3D-IMC吞吐量提升2.0倍，端到端吞吐量提升1.74倍。

Conclusion: CAMP能够显著提升3D-IMC加速器在LLM上的性能表现，同时无需进行硬件改动，是一种有优势的软件解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) incur severe memory-access overheads due to the cost of loading large weight matrices and key-value (KV) caches. Analog In-Memory Computing (AIMC) is an emerging solution that enables computation directly on static weights stored in memory, substantially reducing energy and latency. 3D-IMC further stacks memory arrays into vertical tiers, increasing capacity but revealing a new challenge: how weights are placed in memory determines which matrix-vector multiplications (MVMs) can execute concurrently, affecting latency and throughput. The concurrency aspect of 3D-IMC weight-mapping remains underexplored in the literature, leading to sub-optimal utilization of compute resources on 3D-IMC chips. In this context we propose CAMP (Context-Aware Mapping and Placement), an inference-context-aware weight mapping algorithm for 3D-IMC-based LLM accelerators. CAMP captures the potential resource contention resulting from the architecture, targeted network, and runtime context in a conflict matrix, enabling throughput-optimized weight mapping. This mapping is refined through a profiling-driven feedback loop that efficiently prunes the search space and returns optimized mappings within practical runtimes. CAMP is an offline, software-based solution requiring no hardware changes and improving 3D-IMC compute utilization from 42.4% to 60.3%, delivering up to 2.0x higher 3D-IMC throughput and 1.74x higher end-to-end throughput over state-of-the-art mapping algorithms.

</details>


### [250] [Should You Split Decode? Disaggregation Across Future Models and Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08299)
*Ramyad Hadidi*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 研究发现，随着模型和硬件的发展，解码器分离对系统性能（每千瓦时的服务级吞吐量）的影响并不比前缀/解码分离的优势显著。尽管在某些特定条件下（例如，键值模型方向且共存专家池覆盖多个小规模扩展领域），解码器分离有可能带来优势，但总体来看，前缀和内存带宽以及池的粒度才是决定部署的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言模型（LLM）的发展和加速器技术的更新，对前缀/解码分离（P/D）和解码器分离（AFD）这两种解码方式的效率和成本进行了深入研究，尤其是在资源预约和硬件选择方面的权衡。

Method: 研究人员构建了一个名为SiliconForge的模型，用来评估P/D和P/D+AFD两种方法在不同模型规模和不同加速器类型下的表现，通过相同的管道重叠规则和功耗预算来进行优化。

Result: 研究表明，在大多数情况下，P/D和P/D+AFD在服务级别的吞吐量（每千瓦时）方面并没有显示出明显的性能优势。AFD略微优于P/D主要是在处理键值模型方向的情况，但在更大的模型规模或更快的网络情况下这种优势会减弱。

Conclusion: 因此，研究得出结论，未来的部署应更加关注前缀分配、解码内存带宽和池的粒度等因素，而不是仅仅决定于两种方法的简单分离。灵活的硬件分配有助于减少初始资源承诺带来的效率损失。

Abstract: For large language model (LLM) inference, prefill/decode disaggregation (P/D) is deployed in production to isolate phases with different resource demands. Attention/feed-forward network disaggregation (AFD) splits decode again, but adds communication, placement constraints, and another provisioned pool. We ask whether role-specific placement repays this cost as models and accelerator roadmaps change. Infrastructure is committed before future models and hardware can be benchmarked. We present SiliconForge, a calibrated model that varies the serving decomposition and hardware assignment independently. We compare P/D and P/D+AFD across four model directions, four accelerator classes anchored to current and announced parts, and two fabrics. We co-optimize each design using the same rules for pipeline overlap and the same power budget. Our main comparisons show no robust deployment-level AFD advantage in service-level objective (SLO) goodput per kW. Most nominal estimates favor P/D. The largest AFD lead comes from fitting one more prefill replica, not from a more efficient decode pool. AFD has a nominal lead in the KV-heavy (key-value) model direction when the co-located expert pool spans several small scale-up domains. Larger domains or a fast fabric for both designs weaken that lead. We therefore find that prefill provisioning, decode memory bandwidth, links, and pool granularity decide deployments more than the split alone. A fixed hardware assignment can lose its advantage as models, fabrics, hardware assumptions, and objectives change. Re-optimizing mappings and pool sizes on the same hardware cuts early commitment's median normalized goodput loss from 36% to 9%. We recommend adjustable mappings and pool sizes to let boundary and hardware choices follow model demands.

</details>


### [251] [Scaling Up Analog CIM: A Simulation Framework for Topology-Aware Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08399)
*Yuxin Ren,Danella Zhao,Huanrui Yang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Isobar 平台通过系统级建模来优化 Analog CIM 系统的架构，减少通信时间和端到端延迟，特别是在针对千芯片规模进行扩展时，仍保持能源效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大模型部署需求的增长，为了扩展 CIM 的容量，本文提出 Isobar 平台，通过量化映射拓扑对系统性能的影响，优化权重映射，同时利用 CIM 分解、异构执行和资源建模来评估设计空间。

Method: Isobar 平台采用系统级建模方法，集成 CIM 分解、异构执行和资源建模，提供了可靠的成本估算，使得不同模型和硬件配置下的映射搜索更加有效。

Result: Isobar 平台证明了，相较于最强基线，Gemma-7B 预填充的通信时间减少了 23.3%，端到端延迟降低了 18.6%。同时，系统级模型表明，模拟 CIM 相对于 GPU 在扩展到数千个芯片时仍保持能源效率。

Conclusion: Isobar 平台通过考虑拓扑感知映射设计维度，展示了 CIM 系统可以实现高效执行，特别是在更大规模的部署中仍能保持能源效率。

Abstract: Analog compute-in-memory (CIM) enables energy-efficient AI inference by performing in-situ matrix multiplication. The growing demand for deploying larger models motivates chiplet integration to expand CIM capacity. However, simulation abstractions based on local operator costs and aggregate communication metrics obscure the impact of weight-mapping topology when CIM system scales up. We develop a phase analysis that quantifies how strongly mapping topology affects system performance across hardware configurations and how mapping optimization translates into latency gains. To evaluate and exploit this design space, we present Isobar, a simulation platform integrating CIM decompositions, heterogeneous execution, and resource modeling. Validated against silicon measurements and cycle-accurate simulation, Isobar provides reliable cost estimates that enable effective mapping search across model and hardware configurations. For Gemma-7B prefill, Isobar-guided mapping reduces communication time by 23.3% and end-to-end latency by 18.6% relative to the strongest baseline. Isobar's system-level modeling show that analog CIM retains energy-efficiency potential relative to GPU when scaled to thousands of dies, and identify topology-aware mapping as a key design dimension for efficient execution.

</details>
